The core is well tested and mature.\n12\t\n13\t\n14\t## Introduction ##\n15\t\n16\tThis implementation supports 128 and 256 bit keys. The\n17\timplementation is iterative and process one 128 block at a time. Blocks\n18\tare processed on a word level with 4 S-boxes in the data path. The\n19\tS-boxes for encryption are shared with the key expansion and the core\n20\tcan thus not do key update in parallel with block processing.\n21\t\n22\tThe encipher and decipher block processing datapaths are separated and\n23\tbasically self contained given access to a set of round keys and a\n24\tblock. This makes it possible to hard wire the core to only encipher or\n25\tdecipher operation. This allows the synthesis/build tools to optimize\n26\taway the other functionality which will reduce the size to about\n27\t50%. This has been tested to verify that decryption is removed and the\n28\tcore still works.\n29\t\n30\tFor cipher modes such as CTR, CCM, CMAC, GCM the decryption\n31\tfunctionality in the AES core will never be used and thus the decipher\n32\tblock processing can be removed.\n33\t\n34\tThis is a fairly compact implementation. Further reduction could be\n35\tachived by just having a single S-box. Similarly the performane can be\n36\tincreased by having 8 or even 16 S-boxes which would reduce the number\n37\tof cycles to two cycles for each round.\n38\t\n39\t\n40\t### Contact information ##\n41\t\n42\tAssured provides customer support including customization, integration\n43\tand system development related to the core. The\n52\tbranches available that provides versions of the core are:\n53\t\n54\t\n55\t### on-the-fly-keygen ###\n56\t\n57\tThis version of AES implements the key expansion using an on-the-fly\n58\tmechanism. This allows the initial key expansion to be removed. This\n59\tsaves a number of cycles and also remove almost 1800 registers needed to\n60\tstore the round keys. Note that this version of AES only supports\n61\tencryption. On-the-fly key generation does not work with\n62\tdecryption. This is useful for example in\n70\tan AEAD mode with CBC + CMAC implemented using a single AES core.\n71\t\n72\t\n73\t### cmt-sbox ###\n74\t\n75\tAn experimental version of the core in which the S-box is implemented\n76\tusing circuit minimized logic functions of a ROM table. Load the key to be used by writing to the key register words.\n117\t2. Set the key length by writing to the config register.\n118\t3. Initialize key expansion by writing a one to the init bit in the control register.\n119\t4. Wait for the ready bit in the status register to be cleared and then to be set again. This means that the key expansion has been completed.\n120\t5. Write the cleartext block to the block registers.\n121\t6. Start block processing by writing a one to the next bit in the control register.\n122\t7. Wait for the ready bit in the status register to be cleared and then to be set again. This means that the data block has been processed.\n123\t8. Read out the ciphertext block from the result registers.\n124\t\n125\t\n126\t## FuseSoC\n127\tThis core is supported by the\n128\t[FuseSoC](https://github.com/olofk/fusesoc) core package manager and\n129\tbuild system. Automatic clock gating and scan\n180\tinsertion.\n181\t\n182\t- 8 kCells\n183\t- Aera: 520 x 520 um\n184\t- Good timing margin with no big cells and buffers.\n185\t\n186\t\n187\t## Implementation results - FPGA ##\n188\t\n189\tThe core has been implemented in Altera and Xilinx FPGA devices.\n190\t\n191\t### Altera Cyclone V GX ###\n192\t- 2624 ALMs\n193\t- 3123 Regs\n194\t- 96 MHz\n195\t- 46 cycles/block\n196\t\n197\t\n198\t### Altera Cyclone IV GX ###\n199\t- 7426 LEs\n200\t- 2994 Regs\n201\t- 96 MHz fmax\n202\t- 46 cycles/block\n203\t\n204\tThis means that we can do more than 2 Mblocks/s or 256 Mbps\n205\tperformance.\n206\t\n207\tRemoving the decipher module yields:\n208\t- 5497 LEs\n209\t- 2855 Regs\n210\t- 106 MHz fmax\n211\t- 46 cycles/block\n212\t\n213\t\n214\t### Microchip IGLOO 2 ###\n215\t- Tool: Libero v 12.4\n216\t- Device: M2GL090TS-1FG484I\n217\t- LUTs: 6335\n218\t- SLEs: 1376\n219\t- BRAMs: 8\n220\t- Fmax: 98.5 MHz\n221\t\n222\t\n223\t### Xilinx Spartan6LX-3 ###\n224\t- 2576 slices\n225\t- 3000 regs\n226\t- 100 MHz\n227\t- 46 cycles/block\n228\t\n229\t\n230\t### Xilinx Artix 7 200T-3 ###\n231\t- 2298 slices\n232\t- 2989 regs\n233\t- 97 MHz\n234\t- 46 cycles/block\n235\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9f17580c-3c5d-4208-818a-d71ba3227a15","uuid":"bfab7852-23c0-46d7-ab1e-0eab5c4c67a4","timestamp":"2026-05-03T17:55:59.041Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/app/README.md","content":"[![build-openlane-sky130](https://github.com/secworks/aes/actions/workflows/ci.yml/badge.svg?branch=master&event=push)](https://github.com/secworks/aes/actions/workflows/ci.yml)\n\naes\n===\n\nVerilog implementation of the [symmetric block cipher AES (NIST FIPS 197)](http://csrc.nist.gov/publications/fips/fips197/fips-197.pdf).\n\n\n## Status ##\nThe core is completed, has been used in several FPGA and ASIC\ndesigns. The core is well tested and mature.\n\n\n## Introduction ##\n\nThis implementation supports 128 and 256 bit keys. The\nimplementation is iterative and process one 128 block at a time. Blocks\nare processed on a word level with 4 S-boxes in the data path. The\nS-boxes for encryption are shared with the key expansion and the core\ncan thus not do key update in parallel with block processing.\n\nThe encipher and decipher block processing datapaths are separated and\nbasically self contained given access to a set of round keys and a\nblock. This makes it possible to hard wire the core to only encipher or\ndecipher operation. This allows the synthesis/build tools to optimize\naway the other functionality which will reduce the size to about\n50%. This has been tested to verify that decryption is removed and the\ncore still works.\n\nFor cipher modes such as CTR, CCM, CMAC, GCM the decryption\nfunctionality in the AES core will never be used and thus the decipher\nblock processing can be removed.\n\nThis is a fairly compact implementation. Further reduction could be\nachived by just having a single S-box. Similarly the performane can be\nincreased by having 8 or even 16 S-boxes which would reduce the number\nof cycles to two cycles for each round.\n\n\n### Contact information ##\n\nAssured provides customer support including customization, integration\nand system development related to the core. For more information,\nplease contact [Assured Security\nConsultants](https://www.assured.se/contact).\n\n\n## Branches ##\n\nThere are several branches available that provides different versions of\nthe core. The branches are not planned to be merged into master. The\nbranches available that provides versions of the core are:\n\n\n### on-the-fly-keygen ###\n\nThis version of AES implements the key expansion using an on-the-fly\nmechanism. This allows the initial key expansion to be removed. This\nsaves a number of cycles and also remove almost 1800 registers needed to\nstore the round keys. Note that this version of AES only supports\nencryption. On-the-fly key generation does not work with\ndecryption. Decryption must be handled by the block cipher mode - for\nexample CTR.\n\n\n### dual-keys ###\n\nThis version of AES supports two separate banks of expanded keys to\nallow fast key switching between two keys. This is useful for example in\nan AEAD mode with CBC + CMAC implemented using a single AES core.\n\n\n### cmt-sbox ###\n\nAn experimental version of the core in which the S-box is implemented\nusing circuit minimized logic functions of a ROM table. The specific\ntable used is\n[the 113 gate circuit](http://cs-www.cs.yale.edu/homes/peralta/CircuitStuff/SLP_AES_113.txt) by the [CMT team at Yale](http://cs-www.cs.yale.edu/homes/peralta/CircuitStuff/CMT.html).\n\nSome area and performance results using the cmt_sbox compared to\nmaster:\n\n#### Altera\n- Tool: Quartus Prime 19.1.0\n- Device: Cyclone V (5CGXFC7C7F23C8)\n- master (S-box implemented with a table)\n  - ALMs: 2599\n  - Regs: 3184\n  - Fmax: 93 MHz\n  - aes_sbox: 160 ALUTs\n\n- cmt_sbox\n  - ALMs: 2759\n  - Regs: 3147\n  - Fmax: 69 MHz\n  - aes_sbox: 363 ALUTs\n\n\n#### Xilinx\n- Tool: Vivado 2019.2\n- Device: Kintex-7 (7k70tfbv676-1)\n- master:\n  - LUTs: 3020\n  - FFs: 2992\n  - Fmax: 125 MHz\n\n- cmt_sbox:\n  - LUTs: 2955\n  - FFs: 2992\n  - Fmax: 105 MHz\n\n\n## Core Usage\n\n### Usage sequence:\n1. Load the key to be used by writing to the key register words.\n2. Set the key length by writing to the config register.\n3. Initialize key expansion by writing a one to the init bit in the control register.\n4. Wait for the ready bit in the status register to be cleared and then to be set again. This means that the key expansion has been completed.\n5. Write the cleartext block to the block registers.\n6. Start block processing by writing a one to the next bit in the control register.\n7. Wait for the ready bit in the status register to be cleared and then to be set again. This means that the data block has been processed.\n8. Read out the ciphertext block from the result registers.\n\n\n## FuseSoC\nThis core is supported by the\n[FuseSoC](https://github.com/olofk/fusesoc) core package manager and\nbuild system. Some quick  FuseSoC instructions:\n\ninstall FuseSoC\n~~~\npip install fusesoc\n~~~\n\nCreate and enter a new workspace\n~~~\nmkdir workspace && cd workspace\n~~~\n\nRegister aes as a library in the workspace\n~~~\nfusesoc library add aes /path/to/aes\n~~~\n\n...if repo is available locally or...\n...to get the upstream repo\n~~~\nfusesoc library add aes https://github.com/secworks/aes\n~~~\n\nTo run lint\n~~~\nfusesoc run --target=lint secworks:crypto:aes\n~~~\n\nRun tb_aes testbench\n~~~\nfusesoc run --target=tb_aes secworks:crypto:aes\n~~~\n\nRun with modelsim instead of default tool (icarus)\n~~~\nfusesoc run --target=tb_aes --tool=modelsim secworks:crypto:aes\n~~~\n\nList all targets\n~~~\nfusesoc core show secworks:crypto:aes\n~~~\n\n\n## Implementation results - ASIC ##\n\nThe core has been implemented in standard cell ASIC processes.\n\n### TSMC 180 nm ###\nTarget frequency: 20 MHz\nComplete flow from RTL to placed gates. 0x%01x\",\n173\t               dut.key_mem_new, dut.key_mem_we);\n174\t      $display(\"\");\n175\t\n176\t      if (SHOW_SBOX)\n177\t        begin\n178\t          $display(\"Sbox functionality:\");\n179\t          $display(\"sboxw = 0x%08x\", sbox.sboxw);\n180\t          $display(\"tmp_new_sbox0 = 0x%02x, tmp_new_sbox1 = 0x%02x, tmp_new_sbox2 = 0x%02x, tmp_new_sbox3\",\n181\t                   sbox.tmp_new_sbox0, sbox.tmp_new_sbox1, sbox.tmp_new_sbox2, sbox.tmp_new_sbox3);\n182\t          $display(\"new_sboxw = 0x%08x\", sbox.new_sboxw);\n183\t          $display(\"\");\n184\t        end\n185\t    end\n186\t  endtask // dump_dut_state\n187\t\n188\t\n189\t  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n190\t  // reset_dut()\n191\t  //\n192\t  // Toggle reset to put the DUT into a well known state.\n193\t  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n194\t  task reset_dut;\n195\t    begin\n196\t      $display(\"*** Toggle reset.\");\n197\t      tb_reset_n = 0;\n198\t      #(2 * CLK_PERIOD);\n199\t      tb_reset_n = 1;\n200\t    end\n201\t  endtask // reset_dut\n202\t\n203\t\n204\t  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n205\t  // init_sim()\n206\t  //\n207\t  // Initialize all counters and testbed functionality as well\n208\t  // as setting the DUT inputs to defined values.\n209\t  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n210\t  task init_sim;\n211\t    begin\n212\t      cycle_ctr = 0;\n213\t      error_ctr = 0;\n214\t      tc_ctr    = 0;\n215\t\n216\t      tb_clk     = 0;\n217\t      tb_reset_n = 1;\n218\t      tb_key     = {8{32'h00000000}};\n219\t      tb_keylen  = 0;\n220\t      tb_init    = 0;\n221\t      tb_round   = 4'h0;\n222\t    end\n223\t  endtask // init_sim\n224\t\n225\t\n226\t  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n227\t  // wait_ready()\n228\t  //\n229\t  // Wait for the ready flag in the dut to be set.\n230\t  //\n231\t  // Note: It is the callers responsibility to call the function\n232\t  // when the dut is actively processing and will in fact at some\n233\t  // point set the flag.\n234\t  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n235\t  task wait_ready;\n236\t    begin\n237\t      while (!tb_ready)\n238\t        begin\n239\t          #(CLK_PERIOD);\n240\t        end\n241\t    end\n242\t  endtask // wait_ready\n243\t\n244\t\n245\t  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n246\t  // check_key()\n247\t  //\n248\t  // Check a given key in the dut key memory against a given\n249\t  // expected key.\n250\t  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n251\t  task check_key(input [3 : 0] key_nr, input [127 : 0] expected);\n252\t    begin\n253\t      tb_round = key_nr;\n254\t      #(CLK_PERIOD);\n255\t      if (tb_round_key == expected)\n256\t        begin\n257\t          $display(\"** key 0x%01x matched expected round key.\", key_nr);\n258\t          $display(\"** Got:      0x%016x **\", tb_round_key);\n259\t        end\n260\t      else\n261\t        begin\n262\t          $display(\"** Error: key 0x%01x did not match expected round key. **\", key_nr);\n263\t          $display(\"** Expected: 0x%016x **\", expected);\n264\t          $display(\"** Got:      0x%016x **\", tb_round_key);\n265\t          error_ctr = error_ctr + 1;\n266\t        end\n267\t      $display(\"\");\n268\t    end\n269\t  endtask // check_key\n270\t\n271\t\n272\t  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n273\t  // test_key_128()\n274\t  //\n275\t  // Test 128 bit keys. Due to array problems, the result check\n276\t  // is fairly ugly.\n277\t  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n278\t  task test_key_128(input [255 : 0] key,\n279\t                    input [127 : 0] expected00,\n280\t                    input [127 : 0] expected01,\n281\t                    input [127 : 0] expected02,\n282\t                    input [127 : 0] expected03,\n283\t                    input [127 : 0] expected04,\n284\t                    input [127 : 0] expected05,\n285\t                    input [127 : 0] expected06,\n286\t                    input [127 : 0] expected07,\n287\t                    input [127 : 0] expected08,\n288\t                    input [127 : 0] expected09,\n289\t                    input [127 : 0] expected10\n290\t                   );\n291\t    begin\n292\t      $display(\"** Testing with 128-bit key 0x%16x\", key[255 : 128]);\n293\t      $display(\"\");\n294\t\n295\t      tb_key = key;\n296\t      tb_keylen = AES_128_BIT_KEY;\n297\t      tb_init = 1;\n298\t      #(2 * CLK_PERIOD);\n299\t      tb_init = 0;\n300\t      wait_ready();\n301\t\n302\t      check_key(4'h0, expected00);\n303\t      check_key(4'h1, expected01);\n304\t      check_key(4'h2, expected02);\n305\t      check_key(4'h3, expected03);\n306\t      check_key(4'h4, expected04);\n307\t      check_key(4'h5, expected05);\n308\t      check_key(4'h6, expected06);\n309\t      check_key(4'h7, expected07);\n310\t      check_key(4'h8, expected08);\n311\t      check_key(4'h9, expected09);\n312\t      check_key(4'ha, expected10);\n313\t\n314\t      tc_ctr = tc_ctr + 1;\n315\t    end\n316\t  endtask // test_key_128\n317\t\n318\t\n319\t  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n320\t  // test_key_256()\n321\t  //\n322\t  // Test 256 bit keys. Due to array problems, the result check\n323\t  // is fairly ugly.\n324\t  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n325\t  task test_key_256(input [255 : 0] key,\n326\t input [127 : 0] expected07,\n334\t                    input [127 : 0] expected08,\n335\t                    input [127 : 0] expected09,\n336\t                    input [127 : 0] expected10,\n337\t                    input [127 : 0] expected11,\n338\t                    input [127 : 0] expected12,\n339\t                    input [127 : 0] expected13,\n340\t                    input [127 : 0] expected14\n341\t                   );\n342\t    begin\n343\t      $display(\"** Testing with 256-bit key 0x%32x\", key[255 : 000]);\n344\t      $display(\"\");\n345\t\n346\t      tb_key = key;\n347\t      tb_keylen = AES_256_BIT_KEY;\n348\t      tb_init = 1;\n349\t      #(2 * CLK_PERIOD);\n350\t      tb_init = 0;\n351\t\n352\t      wait_ready();\n353\t\n354\t      check_key(4'h0, expected00);\n355\t      check_key(4'h1, expected01);\n356\t      check_key(4'h2, expected02);\n357\t      check_key(4'h3, expected03);\n358\t      check_key(4'h4, expected04);\n359\t      check_key(4'h5, expected05);\n360\t      check_key(4'h6, expected06);\n361\t      check_key(4'h7, expected07);\n362\t      check_key(4'h8, expected08);\n363\t      check_key(4'h9, expected09);\n364\t gm13()\n272\t    #\n273\t    # The specific Galois Multiplication by 13 for a given byte.\n274\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n275\t    def gm13(self, b):\n276\t        return self.gm8(b) ^ self.gm4(b) ^ b\n277\t\n278\t\n279\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n280\t    # gm14()\n281\t    #\n282\t    # The specific Galois Multiplication by 14 for a given byte.\n283\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n284\t    def gm14(self, b):\n285\t        return self.gm8(b) ^ self.gm4(b) ^ self.gm2(b)\n286\t\n287\t\n288\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n289\t    # substw()\n290\t    #\n291\t    # Returns a 32-bit word in which each of the bytes in the\n292\t    # given 32-bit word has been used as lookup into the AES S-box.\n293\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n294\t    def substw(self, w):\n295\t        (b0, b1, b2, b3) = self.w2b(w)\n296\t        s0 = self.sbox[b0]\n297\t        s1 = self.sbox[b1]\n298\t        s2 = self.sbox[b2]\n299\t        s3 = self.sbox[b3]\n300\t        res = self.b2w(s0, s1, s2, s3)\n301\t\n302\t        if (self.VERBOSE):\n303\t            print(\"Inside substw:\")\n304\t            print(\"b0 = 0x%02x, b1 = 0x%02x, b2 = 0x%02x, b3 = 0x%02x\" %\n305\t                  (b0, b1, b2, b3))\n306\t            print(\"s0 = 0x%02x, s1 = 0x%02x, s2 = 0x%02x, s3 = 0x%02x\" %\n307\t                  (s0, s1, s2, s3))\n308\t            print(\"res = 0x%08x\" % (res))\n309\t        return res\n310\t\n311\t\n312\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n313\t    # inv_substw()\n314\t    #\n315\t    # Returns a 32-bit word in which each of the bytes in the\n316\t    # given 32-bit word has been used as lookup into\n317\t    # the inverse AES S-box.\n318\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n319\t    def inv_substw(self, w):\n320\t        (b0, b1, b2, b3) = self.w2b(w)\n321\t        s0 = self.inv_sbox[b0]\n322\t        s1 = self.inv_sbox[b1]\n323\t        s2 = self.inv_sbox[b2]\n324\t        s3 = self.inv_sbox[b3]\n325\t        res = self.b2w(s0, s1, s2, s3)\n326\t\n327\t        if (self.VERBOSE):\n328\t            print(\"Inside inv_substw:\")\n329\t            print(\"b0 = 0x%02x, b1 = 0x%02x, b2 = 0x%02x, b3 = 0x%02x\" %\n330\t                (b0, b1, b2, b3))\n331\t            print(\"s0 = 0x%02x, s1 = 0x%02x, s2 = 0x%02x, s3 = 0x%02x\" %\n332\t                (s0, s1, s2, s3))\n333\t            print(\"res = 0x%08x\" % (res))\n334\t        return res\n335\t\n336\t\n337\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n338\t    # rolx()\n339\t    #\n340\t    # Rotate the given 32 bit word x bits left.\n341\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n342\t    def rolx(self, w, x):\n343\t        return ((w << x) | (w >> (32 - x))) & 0xffffffff\n344\t\n345\t\n346\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n347\t    # next_128bit_key()\n348\t    #\n349\t    # Generate the next four key words for aes-128 based on given\n350\t    # rcon and previous key words.\n351\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n352\t    def next_128bit_key(self, prev_key, rcon):\n353\t        (w0, w1, w2, w3) = prev_key\n354\t\n355\t        rol = self.rolx(w3, 8)\n356\t        subst = self.substw(rol)\n357\t        t = subst ^ (rcon << 24)\n358\t\n359\t        k0 = w0 ^ t\n360\t        k1 = w1 ^ w0 ^ t\n361\t        k2 = w2 ^ w1 ^ w0 ^ t\n362\t        k3 = w3 ^ w2 ^ w1 ^ w0 ^ t\n363\t\n364\t        if (self.VERBOSE):\n365\t            print(\"Inside next 128bit key:\")\n366\t            print(\"w0 = 0x%08x, w1 = 0x%08x, w2 = 0x%08x, w3 = 0x%08x\" %\n367\t                  (w0, w1, w2, w3))\n368\t            print(\"rol = 0x%08x, subst = 0x%08x, rcon = 0x%02x, t = 0x%08x\" %\n369\t                  (rol, subst, rcon, t))\n370\t            print(\"k0 = 0x%08x, k1 = 0x%08x, k2 = 0x%08x, k3 = 0x%08x\" %\n371\t                  (k0, k1, k2, k3))\n372\t        return (k0, k1, k2, k3)\n373\t\n374\t\n375\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n376\t    # key_gen128()\n377\t    #\n378\t    # Generating the keys for 128 bit keys.\n379\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n380\t    def key_gen128(self, key):\n381\t        print(\"Doing the 128 bit key expansion\")\n382\t\n383\t        round_keys = []\n384\t        round_keys.append(key)\n385\t\n386\t        for i in range(10):\n387\t            round_keys.append(self.next_128bit_key(round_keys[i], self.get_rcon(i + 1)))\n388\t\n389\t        if (self.VERBOSE):\n390\t            print(\"Input key:\")\n391\t            self.print_block(key)\n392\t            print(\"\")\n393\t\n394\t            print(\"Generated keys:\")\n395\t            for k in round_keys:\n396\t                self.print_block(k)\n397\t            print(\"\")\n398\t\n399\t        return round_keys\n400\t\n401\t\n402\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n403\t    # next_256bit_key_a()\n404\t    #\n405\t    # Generate the next four key words for aes-256 using algorithm A\n406\t    # based on given rcon and the previous two keys.\n407\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n408\t    def next_256it_key_a(self, key0, key1, rcon):\n409\t        (w0, w1, w2, w3) = key0\n410\t        (w4, w5, w6, w7) = key1\n411\t\n412\t        sw = self.substw(self.rolx(w7, 8))\n413\t        rw = (rcon << 24)\n414\t        t = sw ^ rw\n415\t\n416\t        k0 = w0 ^ t\n417\t        k1 = w1 ^ w0 ^ t\n418\t        k2 = w2 ^ w1 ^ w0 ^ t\n419\t        k3 = w3 ^ w2 ^ w1 ^ w0 ^ t\n420\t\n421\t        if (self.DUMP_VARS):\n422\t            print(\"next_256bit_key_a:\")\n423\t            print(\"w0 = 0x%08x, w0 = 0x%08x, w0 = 0x%08x, w0 = 0x%08x\" % (w0, w1, w2, w3))\n424\t            print(\"w4 = 0x%08x, w5 = 0x%08x, w6 = 0x%08x, w7 = 0x%08x\" % (w4, w5, w6, w7))\n425\t            print(\"t = 0x%08x, sw = 0x%08x, rw = 0x%08x\" % (t, sw, rw))\n426\t            print(\"k0 = 0x%08x, k0 = 0x%08x, k0 = 0x%08x, k0 = 0x%08x\" % (k0, k1, k2, k3))\n427\t            print(\"\")\n428\t\n429\t        return (k0, k1, k2, k3)\n430\t\n431\t\n432\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n433\t    # next_256bit_key_b()\n434\t    #\n435\t    # Generate the next four key words for aes-256 using algorithm B\n436\t    # based on given previous eight keywords.\n437\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n438\t    def next_256it_key_b(self, key0, key1):\n439\t        (w0, w1, w2, w3) = key0\n440\t        (w4, w5, w6, w7) = key1\n441\t\n442\t        t = self.substw(w7)\n443\t\n444\t        k0 = w0 ^ t\n445\t        k1 = w1 ^ w0 ^ t\n446\t        k2 = w2 ^ w1 ^ w0 ^ t\n447\t        k3 = w3 ^ w2 ^ w1 ^ w0 ^ t\n448\t\n449\t        if (self.DUMP_VARS):\n450\t            print(\"next_256bit_key_b:\")\n451\t            print(\"w0 = 0x%08x, w0 = 0x%08x, w0 = 0x%08x, w0 = 0x%08x\" % (w0, w1, w2, w3))\n452\t            print(\"w4 = 0x%08x, w5 = 0x%08x, w6 = 0x%08x, w7 = 0x%08x\" % (w4, w5, w6, w7))\n453\t            print(\"t = 0x%08x\" % (t))\n454\t            print(\"k0 = 0x%08x, k0 = 0x%08x, k0 = 0x%08x, k0 = 0x%08x\" % (k0, k1, k2, k3))\n455\t            print(\"\")\n456\t\n457\t        return (k0, k1, k2, k3)\n458\t\n459\t\n460\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n461\t    # key_gen256()\n462\t    #\n463\t    # Generating the keys for 256 bit keys.\n464\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n465\t    def key_gen256(self, key):\n466\t        round_keys = []\n467\t        (k0, k1, k2, k3, k4, k5, k6, k7) = key\n468\t\n469\t        round_keys.append((k0, k1, k2, k3))\n470\t        round_keys.append((k4, k5, k6, k7))\n471\t\n472\t        j = 1\n473\t        for i in range(0, (self.AES_256_ROUNDS - 2), 2):\n474\t            k = self.next_256it_key_a(round_keys[i], round_keys[i + 1], self.get_rcon(j))\n475\t            round_keys.append(k)\n476\t            k = self.next_256it_key_b(round_keys[i + 1], round_keys[i + 2])\n477\t            round_keys.append(k)\n478\t            j += 1\n479\t\n480\t        # One final key needs to be generated.\n481\t        k = self.next_256it_key_a(round_keys[12], round_keys[13], self.get_rcon(7))\n482\t        round_keys.append(k)\n483\t\n484\t        if (self.VERBOSE):\n485\t            print(\"Input key:\")\n486\t            self.print_block((k0, k1, k2, k3))\n487\t            self.print_block((k4, k5, k6, k7))\n488\t            print(\"\")\n489\t\n490\t            print(\"Generated keys:\")\n491\t            for k in round_keys:\n492\t                self.print_block(k)\n493\t            print(\"\")\n494\t\n495\t        return round_keys\n496\t\n497\t\n498\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n499\t    # get_rcon()\n500\t    #\n501\t    # Function implementation of rcon. Calculates rcon for a\n502\t    # given round. This could be implemented as an iterator.\n503\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n504\t    def get_rcon(self, round):\n505\t        rcon = 0x8d\n506\t\n507\t        for i in range(0, round):\n508\t            rcon = ((rcon << 1) ^ (0x11b & - (rcon >> 7))) & 0xff\n509\t\n510\t        return rcon\n511\t\n512\t\n513\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n514\t    # addroundkey()\n515\t    #\n516\t    # AES AddRoundKey block operation.\n517\t    # Perform XOR combination of the given block and the given key.\n518\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n519\t    def addroundkey(self, key, block):\n520\t        (w0, w1, w2, w3) = block\n521\t        (k0, k1, k2, k3) = key\n522\t\n523\t        res_block = (w0 ^ k0, w1 ^ k1, w2 ^ k2, w3 ^ k3)\n524\t\n525\t        if (self.VERBOSE):\n526\t            print(\"AddRoundKey key, block in and block out:\")\n527\t            self.print_block(key)\n528\t            self.print_block(block)\n529\t            self.print_block(res_block)\n530\t            print(\"\")\n531\t\n532\t        return res_block\n533\t\n534\t\n535\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n536\t    # mixw()\n537\t    #\n538\t    # Perform bit mixing of the given words.\n539\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n540\t    def mixw(self, w):\n541\t        (b0, b1, b2, b3) = self.w2b(w)\n542\t\n543\t        mb0 = self.gm2(b0) ^ self.gm3(b1) ^ b2           ^ b3\n544\t        mb1 = b0           ^ self.gm2(b1) ^ self.gm3(b2) ^ b3\n545\t        mb2 = b0           ^ b1           ^ self.gm2(b2) ^ self.gm3(b3)\n546\t        mb3 = self.gm3(b0) ^ b1           ^ b2           ^ self.gm2(b3)\n547\t\n548\t        return self.b2w(mb0, mb1, mb2, mb3)\n549\t\n550\t\n551\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n552\t    # mixcolumns()\n553\t    #\n554\t    # AES MixColumns on the given block.\n555\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n556\t    def mixcolumns(self, block):\n557\t        (c0, c1, c2, c3) = block\n558\t\n559\t        mc0 = self.mixw(c0)\n560\t        mc1 = self.mixw(c1)\n561\t        mc2 = self.mixw(c2)\n562\t        mc3 = self.mixw(c3)\n563\t\n564\t        res_block = (mc0, mc1, mc2, mc3)\n565\t\n566\t        if (self.VERBOSE):\n567\t            print(\"MixColumns block in and block out:\")\n568\t            self.print_block(block)\n569\t            self.print_block(res_block)\n570\t            print(\"\")\n571\t\n572\t        return res_block\n573\t\n574\t\n575\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n576\t    # subbytes()\n577\t    #\n578\t    # AES SubBytes operation on the given block.\n579\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n580\t    def subbytes(self, block):\n581\t        (w0, w1, w2, w3) = block\n582\t\n583\t        res_block = (self.substw(w0), self.substw(w1),\n584\t                    self.substw(w2), self.substw(w3))\n585\t\n586\t        if (self.VERBOSE):\n587\t            print(\"SubBytes block in and block out:\")\n588\t            self.print_block(block)\n589\t            self.print_block(res_block)\n590\t            print(\"\")\n591\t\n592\t        return res_block\n593\t\n594\t\n595\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n596\t    # shiftrows()\n597\t    #\n598\t    # AES ShiftRows block operation.\n599\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n600\t    def shiftrows(self, block):\n601\t        (w0, w1, w2, w3) = block\n602\t\n603\t        c0 = self.w2b(w0)\n604\t        c1 = self.w2b(w1)\n605\t        c2 = self.w2b(w2)\n606\t        c3 = self.w2b(w3)\n607\t\n608\t        ws0 = self.b2w(c0[0], c1[1],  c2[2],  c3[3])\n609\t        ws1 = self.b2w(c1[0], c2[1],  c3[2],  c0[3])\n610\t        ws2 = self.b2w(c2[0], c3[1],  c0[2],  c1[3])\n611\t        ws3 = self.b2w(c3[0], c0[1],  c1[2],  c2[3])\n612\t\n613\t        res_block = (ws0, ws1, ws2, ws3)\n614\t\n615\t        if (self.VERBOSE):\n616\t            print(\"ShiftRows block in and block out:\")\n617\t            self.print_block(block)\n618\t            self.print_block(res_block)\n619\t            print(\"\")\n620\t\n621\t        return res_block\n622\t\n623\t\n624\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n625\t    # aes_encipher()\n626\t    #\n627\t    # Perform AES encipher operation for the given block using the\n628\t    # given key length.\n629\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n630\t    def aes_encipher_block(self, key, block):\n631\t        tmp_block = block[:]\n632\t\n633\t        # Get round keys based on the given key.\n634\t        if len(key) == 4:\n635\t            round_keys = self.key_gen128(key)\n636\t            num_rounds = self.AES_128_ROUNDS\n637\t        else:\n638\t            round_keys = self.key_gen256(key)\n639\t            num_rounds = self.AES_256_ROUNDS\n640\t\n641\t        # Init round\n642\t        print(\"  Initial AddRoundKeys round.\")\n643\t        tmp_block4 = self.addroundkey(round_keys[0], block)\n644\t\n645\t        # Main rounds\n646\t        for i in range(1 , (num_rounds)):\n647\t            print(\"\")\n648\t            print(\"  Round %02d\" % i)\n649\t            print(\"  ---------\")\n650\t\n651\t            tmp_block1 = self.subbytes(tmp_block4)\n652\t            tmp_block2 = self.shiftrows(tmp_block1)\n653\t            tmp_block3 = self.mixcolumns(tmp_block2)\n654\t            tmp_block4 = self.addroundkey(round_keys[i], tmp_block3)\n655\t\n656\t\n657\t        # Final round\n658\t        print(\"  Final round.\")\n659\t        tmp_block1 = self.subbytes(tmp_block4)\n660\t        tmp_block2 = self.shiftrows(tmp_block1)\n661\t        tmp_block3 = self.addroundkey(round_keys[num_rounds], tmp_block2)\n662\t\n663\t        return tmp_block3\n664\t\n665\t\n666\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n667\t    # inv_mixw()\n668\t    #\n669\t    # Perform inverse bit mixing of the given words.\n670\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n671\t    def inv_mixw(self, w):\n672\t        (b0, b1, b2, b3) = self.w2b(w)\n673\t\n674\t        mb0 = self.gm14(b0) ^ self.gm11(b1) ^ self.gm13(b2) ^ self.gm09(b3)\n675\t        mb1 = self.gm09(b0) ^ self.gm14(b1) ^ self.gm11(b2) ^ self.gm13(b3)\n676\t        mb2 = self.gm13(b0) ^ self.gm09(b1) ^ self.gm14(b2) ^ self.gm11(b3)\n677\t        mb3 = self.gm11(b0) ^ self.gm13(b1) ^ self.gm09(b2) ^ self.gm14(b3)\n678\t\n679\t        return self.b2w(mb0, mb1, mb2, mb3)\n680\t\n681\t\n682\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n683\t    # inv_mixcolumns()\n684\t    #\n685\t    # AES Inverse MixColumns on the given block.\n686\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n687\t    def inv_mixcolumns(self, block):\n688\t        (c0, c1, c2, c3) = block\n689\t\n690\t        mc0 =       if (self.VERBOSE):\n698\t            print(\"Inverse MixColumns block in and block out:\")\n699\t            self.print_block(block)\n700\t            self.print_block(res_block)\n701\t            print(\"\")\n702\t\n703\t        return res_block\n704\t\n705\t\n706\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n707\t    # inv_shiftrows()\n708\t    #\n709\t    # AES inverse ShiftRows block operation.\n710\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n711\t    def inv_shiftrows(self, block):\n712\t        (w0, w1, w2, w3) =     res_block = (ws0, ws1, ws2, ws3)\n725\t\n726\t        if (self.VERBOSE):\n727\t            print(\"Inverse ShiftRows block in and block out:\")\n728\t            self.print_block(block)\n729\t            self.print_block(res_block)\n730\t            print(\"\")\n731\t\n732\t        return res_block\n733\t\n734\t\n735\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n736\t    # inv_subbytes()\n737\t    #\n738\t    # AES inverse SubBytes operation on the given block.\n739\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n740\t    def inv_subbytes(self, block):\n741\t        (w0, w1, w2, w3) = block\n742\t\n743\t        res_block = #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n756\t    # aes_decipher()\n757\t    #\n758\t    # Perform AES decipher operation for the given block\n759\t    # using the given key length.\n760\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n761\t    def aes_decipher_block(self, key, block):\n762\t        tmp_block = block[:]\n763\t\n764\t        # Get round keys based on the given key.\n765\t        if len(key) == 4:\n766\t            round_keys = self.key_gen128(key)\n767\t            num_rounds = self.AES_128_ROUNDS\n768\t        else:\n769\t            round_keys = self.key_gen256(key)\n770\t            num_rounds = self.AES_256_ROUNDS\n771\t\n772\t            # Initial round\n773\t        print(\"  Initial, partial round.\")\n774\t        tmp_block1 = self.addroundkey(round_keys[len(round_keys) - 1], tmp_block)\n775\t        tmp_block2 = self.inv_shiftrows(tmp_block1)\n776\t        tmp_block4 = self.inv_subbytes(tmp_block2)\n777\t\n778\t        # Main rounds\n779\t        for i in range(1 , (num_rounds)):\n780\t            print(\"\")\n781\t            print(\"  Round %02d\" % i)\n782\t    print(\"  Final AddRoundKeys round.\")\n791\t        res_block = self.addroundkey(round_keys[0], tmp_block4)\n792\t\n793\t        return res_block\n794\t\n795\t\n796\t    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n797\t    # test_mixcolumns()\n798\t    #\n799\t    # Test the mixcolumns and inverse mixcolumns operations using\n800\t    # some #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def __init__(self, verbose = True, dump_vars = True):\n        self.VERBOSE = verbose\n        self.DUMP_VARS = dump_vars\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # check_block()\n    #\n    # Check and report if a result block matches expected block.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def check_block(self, expected, result):\n        if (expected[0] == result[0]) and  (expected[1] == result[1]) and\\\n          (expected[2] == result[2]) and  (expected[3] == result[3]):\n            print(\"OK. Result matches expected.\")\n            print(\"\")\n            return 0\n\n        else:\n            print(\"ERROR. Result does not match expected.\")\n            print(\"Expected:\")\n            self.print_block(expected)\n            print(\"Got:\")\n            self.print_block(result)\n            print(\"\")\n            return 1\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # print_bytekeys()\n    #\n    # Print a set of round keys given as an array of bytes.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def print_bytekeys(self, keys):\n        i = 0\n        print(\"Number of round keys: %d\" % (int(len(keys) / 16)))\n        while i < (len(keys) - 1):\n            for j in range(16):\n                print(\"0x%02x \" % keys[i + j], end=\"\")\n            print(\"\")\n            i += 16\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # print_block()\n    #\n    # Print the given block as four 32 bit words.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def print_block(self, block):\n        (w0, w1, w2, w3) = block\n        print(\"0x%08x, 0x%08x, 0x%08x, 0x%08x\" % (w0, w1, w2, w3))\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # print_key()\n    #\n    # Print the given key as on or two sets of four 32 bit words.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def print_key(self, key):\n        if len(key) == 8:\n            (k0, k1, k2, k3, k4, k5, k6, k7) = key\n            self.print_block((k0, k1, k2, k3))\n            self.print_block((k4, k5, k6, k7))\n        else:\n            self.print_block(key)\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # b2w()\n    #\n    # Create a word from the given bytes.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def b2w(self, b0, b1, b2, b3):\n        return (b0 << 24) + (b1 << 16) + (b2 << 8) + b3\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # w2b()\n    #\n    # Extracts the bytes in a word.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def w2b(self, w):\n        b0 = w >> 24\n        b1 = w >> 16 & 0xff\n        b2 = w >> 8 & 0xff\n        b3 = w & 0xff\n        return (b0, b1, b2, b3)\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # gm2()\n    #\n    # The specific Galois Multiplication by two for a given byte.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def gm2(self, b):\n        return ((b << 1) ^ (0x1b & ((b >> 7) * 0xff))) & 0xff\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # gm3()\n    #\n    # The specific Galois Multiplication by three for a given byte.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def gm3(self, b):\n        return self.gm2(b) ^ b\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # gm4()\n    #\n    # The specific Galois Multiplication by four for a given byte.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def gm4(self, b):\n        return self.gm2(self.gm2(b))\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # gm8()\n    #\n    # The specific Galois Multiplication by eight for a given byte.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def gm8(self, b):\n        return self.gm2(self.gm4(b))\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # gm09()\n    #\n    # The specific Galois Multiplication by nine for a given byte.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def gm09(self, b):\n        return self.gm8(b) ^ b\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # gm11()\n    #\n    # The specific Galois Multiplication by 11 for a given byte.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def gm11(self, b):\n        return self.gm8(b) ^ self.gm2(b) ^ b\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # gm13()\n    #\n    # The specific Galois Multiplication by 13 for a given byte.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def gm13(self, b):\n        return self.gm8(b) ^ self.gm4(b) ^ b\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # gm14()\n    #\n    # The specific Galois Multiplication by 14 for a given byte.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def gm14(self, b):\n        return self.gm8(b) ^ self.gm4(b) ^ self.gm2(b)\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # substw()\n    #\n    # Returns a 32-bit word in which each of the bytes in the\n    # given 32-bit word has been used as lookup into the AES S-box.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def substw(self, w):\n        (b0, b1, b2, b3) = self.w2b(w)\n        s0 = self.sbox[b0]\n        s1 = self.sbox[b1]\n        s2 = self.sbox[b2]\n        s3 = self.sbox[b3]\n        res = self.b2w(s0, s1, s2, s3)\n\n        if (self.VERBOSE):\n            print(\"Inside substw:\")\n            print(\"b0 = 0x%02x, b1 = 0x%02x, b2 = 0x%02x, b3 = 0x%02x\" %\n                  (b0, b1, b2, b3))\n            print(\"s0 = 0x%02x, s1 = 0x%02x, s2 = 0x%02x, s3 = 0x%02x\" %\n                  (s0, s1, s2, s3))\n            print(\"res = 0x%08x\" % (res))\n        return res\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # inv_substw()\n    #\n    # Returns a 32-bit word in which each of the bytes in the\n    # given 32-bit word has been used as lookup into\n    # the inverse AES S-box.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def inv_substw(self, w):\n        (b0, b1, b2, b3) = self.w2b(w)\n        s0 = self.inv_sbox[b0]\n        s1 = self.inv_sbox[b1]\n        s2 = self.inv_sbox[b2]\n        s3 = self.inv_sbox[b3]\n        res = self.b2w(s0, s1, s2, s3)\n\n        if (self.VERBOSE):\n            print(\"Inside inv_substw:\")\n            print(\"b0 = 0x%02x, b1 = 0x%02x, b2 = 0x%02x, b3 = 0x%02x\" %\n                (b0, b1, b2, b3))\n            print(\"s0 = 0x%02x, s1 = 0x%02x, s2 = 0x%02x, s3 = 0x%02x\" %\n                (s0, s1, s2, s3))\n            print(\"res = 0x%08x\" % (res))\n        return res\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # rolx()\n    #\n    # Rotate the given 32 bit word x bits left.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def rolx(self, w, x):\n        return ((w << x) | (w >> (32 - x))) & 0xffffffff\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # next_128bit_key()\n    #\n    # Generate the next four key words for aes-128 based on given\n    # rcon and previous key words.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def next_128bit_key(self, prev_key, rcon):\n        (w0, w1, w2, w3) = prev_key\n\n        rol = self.rolx(w3, 8)\n        subst = self.substw(rol)\n        t = subst ^ (rcon << 24)\n\n        k0 = w0 ^ t\n        k1 = w1 ^ w0 ^ t\n        k2 = w2 ^ w1 ^ w0 ^ t\n        k3 = w3 ^ w2 ^ w1 ^ w0 ^ t\n\n        if (self.VERBOSE):\n            print(\"Inside next 128bit key:\")\n            print(\"w0 = 0x%08x, w1 = 0x%08x, w2 = 0x%08x, w3 = 0x%08x\" %\n                  (w0, w1, w2, w3))\n            print(\"rol = 0x%08x, subst = 0x%08x, rcon = 0x%02x, t = 0x%08x\" %\n                  (rol, subst, rcon, t))\n            print(\"k0 = 0x%08x, k1 = 0x%08x, k2 = 0x%08x, k3 = 0x%08x\" %\n                  (k0, k1, k2, k3))\n        return (k0, k1, k2, k3)\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # key_gen128()\n    #\n    # Generating the keys for 128 bit keys.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def key_gen128(self, key):\n        print(\"Doing the 128 bit key expansion\")\n\n        round_keys = []\n        round_keys.append(key)\n\n        for i in range(10):\n            round_keys.append(self.next_128bit_key(round_keys[i], self.get_rcon(i + 1)))\n\n        if (self.VERBOSE):\n            print(\"Input key:\")\n            self.print_block(key)\n            print(\"\")\n\n            print(\"Generated keys:\")\n            for k in round_keys:\n                self.print_block(k)\n            print(\"\")\n\n        return round_keys\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # next_256bit_key_a()\n    #\n    # Generate the next four key words for aes-256 using algorithm A\n    # based on given rcon and the previous two keys.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def next_256it_key_a(self, key0, key1, rcon):\n        (w0, w1, w2, w3) = key0\n        (w4, w5, w6, w7) = key1\n\n        sw = self.substw(self.rolx(w7, 8))\n        rw = (rcon << 24)\n        t = sw ^ rw\n\n        k0 = w0 ^ t\n        k1 = w1 ^ w0 ^ t\n        k2 = w2 ^ w1 ^ w0 ^ t\n        k3 = w3 ^ w2 ^ w1 ^ w0 ^ t\n\n        if (self.DUMP_VARS):\n            print(\"next_256bit_key_a:\")\n            print(\"w0 = 0x%08x, w0 = 0x%08x, w0 = 0x%08x, w0 = 0x%08x\" % (w0, w1, w2, w3))\n            print(\"w4 = 0x%08x, w5 = 0x%08x, w6 = 0x%08x, w7 = 0x%08x\" % (w4, w5, w6, w7))\n            print(\"t = 0x%08x, sw = 0x%08x, rw = 0x%08x\" % (t, sw, rw))\n            print(\"k0 = 0x%08x, k0 = 0x%08x, k0 = 0x%08x, k0 = 0x%08x\" % (k0, k1, k2, k3))\n            print(\"\")\n\n        return (k0, k1, k2, k3)\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # next_256bit_key_b()\n    #\n    # Generate the next four key words for aes-256 using algorithm B\n    # based on given previous eight keywords.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def next_256it_key_b(self, key0, key1):\n        (w0, w1, w2, w3) = key0\n        (w4, w5, w6, w7) = key1\n\n        t = self.substw(w7)\n\n        k0 = w0 ^ t\n        k1 = w1 ^ w0 ^ t\n        k2 = w2 ^ w1 ^ w0 ^ t\n        k3 = w3 ^ w2 ^ w1 ^ w0 ^ t\n\n        if (self.DUMP_VARS):\n            print(\"next_256bit_key_b:\")\n            print(\"w0 = 0x%08x, w0 = 0x%08x, w0 = 0x%08x, w0 = 0x%08x\" % (w0, w1, w2, w3))\n            print(\"w4 = 0x%08x, w5 = 0x%08x, w6 = 0x%08x, w7 = 0x%08x\" % (w4, w5, w6, w7))\n            print(\"t = 0x%08x\" % (t))\n            print(\"k0 = 0x%08x, k0 = 0x%08x, k0 = 0x%08x, k0 = 0x%08x\" % (k0, k1, k2, k3))\n            print(\"\")\n\n        return (k0, k1, k2, k3)\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # key_gen256()\n    #\n    # Generating the keys for 256 bit keys.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def key_gen256(self, key):\n        round_keys = []\n        (k0, k1, k2, k3, k4, k5, k6, k7) = key\n\n        round_keys.append((k0, k1, k2, k3))\n        round_keys.append((k4, k5, k6, k7))\n\n        j = 1\n        for i in range(0, (self.AES_256_ROUNDS - 2), 2):\n            k = self.next_256it_key_a(round_keys[i], round_keys[i + 1], self.get_rcon(j))\n            round_keys.append(k)\n            k = self.next_256it_key_b(round_keys[i + 1], round_keys[i + 2])\n            round_keys.append(k)\n            j += 1\n\n        # One final key needs to be generated.\n        k = self.next_256it_key_a(round_keys[12], round_keys[13], self.get_rcon(7))\n        round_keys.append(k)\n\n        if (self.VERBOSE):\n            print(\"Input key:\")\n            self.print_block((k0, k1, k2, k3))\n            self.print_block((k4, k5, k6, k7))\n            print(\"\")\n\n            print(\"Generated keys:\")\n            for k in round_keys:\n                self.print_block(k)\n            print(\"\")\n\n        return round_keys\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # get_rcon()\n    #\n    # Function implementation of rcon. Calculates rcon for a\n    # given round. This could be implemented as an iterator.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def get_rcon(self, round):\n        rcon = 0x8d\n\n        for i in range(0, round):\n            rcon = ((rcon << 1) ^ (0x11b & - (rcon >> 7))) & 0xff\n\n        return rcon\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # addroundkey()\n    #\n    # AES AddRoundKey block operation.\n    # Perform XOR combination of the given block and the given key.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def addroundkey(self, key, block):\n        (w0, w1, w2, w3) = block\n        (k0, k1, k2, k3) = key\n\n        res_block = (w0 ^ k0, w1 ^ k1, w2 ^ k2, w3 ^ k3)\n\n        if (self.VERBOSE):\n            print(\"AddRoundKey key, block in and block out:\")\n            self.print_block(key)\n            self.print_block(block)\n            self.print_block(res_block)\n            print(\"\")\n\n        return res_block\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # mixw()\n    #\n    # Perform bit mixing of the given words.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def mixw(self, w):\n        (b0, b1, b2, b3) = self.w2b(w)\n\n        mb0 = self.gm2(b0) ^ self.gm3(b1) ^ b2           ^ b3\n        mb1 = b0           ^ self.gm2(b1) ^ self.gm3(b2) ^ b3\n        mb2 = b0           ^ b1           ^ self.gm2(b2) ^ self.gm3(b3)\n        mb3 = self.gm3(b0) ^ b1           ^ b2           ^ self.gm2(b3)\n\n        return self.b2w(mb0, mb1, mb2, mb3)\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # mixcolumns()\n    #\n    # AES MixColumns on the given block.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def mixcolumns(self, block):\n        (c0, c1, c2, c3) = block\n\n        mc0 = self.mixw(c0)\n        mc1 = self.mixw(c1)\n        mc2 = self.mixw(c2)\n        mc3 = self.mixw(c3)\n\n        res_block = (mc0, mc1, mc2, mc3)\n\n        if (self.VERBOSE):\n            print(\"MixColumns block in and block out:\")\n            self.print_block(block)\n            self.print_block(res_block)\n            print(\"\")\n\n        return res_block\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # subbytes()\n    #\n    # AES SubBytes operation on the given block.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def subbytes(self, block):\n        (w0, w1, w2, w3) = block\n\n        res_block = (self.substw(w0), self.substw(w1),\n                    self.substw(w2), self.substw(w3))\n\n        if (self.VERBOSE):\n            print(\"SubBytes block in and block out:\")\n            self.print_block(block)\n            self.print_block(res_block)\n            print(\"\")\n\n        return res_block\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # shiftrows()\n    #\n    # AES ShiftRows block operation.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def shiftrows(self, block):\n        (w0, w1, w2, w3) = block\n\n        c0 = self.w2b(w0)\n        c1 = self.w2b(w1)\n        c2 = self.w2b(w2)\n        c3 = self.w2b(w3)\n\n        ws0 = self.b2w(c0[0], c1[1],  c2[2],  c3[3])\n        ws1 = self.b2w(c1[0], c2[1],  c3[2],  c0[3])\n        ws2 = self.b2w(c2[0], c3[1],  c0[2],  c1[3])\n        ws3 = self.b2w(c3[0], c0[1],  c1[2],  c2[3])\n\n        res_block = (ws0, ws1, ws2, ws3)\n\n        if (self.VERBOSE):\n            print(\"ShiftRows block in and block out:\")\n            self.print_block(block)\n            self.print_block(res_block)\n            print(\"\")\n\n        return res_block\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # aes_encipher()\n    #\n    # Perform AES encipher operation for the given block using the\n    # given key length.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def aes_encipher_block(self, key, block):\n        tmp_block = block[:]\n\n        # Get round keys based on the given key.\n        if len(key) == 4:\n            round_keys = self.key_gen128(key)\n            num_rounds = self.AES_128_ROUNDS\n        else:\n            round_keys = self.key_gen256(key)\n            num_rounds = self.AES_256_ROUNDS\n\n        # Init round\n        print(\"  Initial AddRoundKeys round.\")\n        tmp_block4 = self.addroundkey(round_keys[0], block)\n\n        # Main rounds\n        for i in range(1 , (num_rounds)):\n            print(\"\")\n            print(\"  Round %02d\" % i)\n            print(\"  ---------\")\n\n            tmp_block1 = self.subbytes(tmp_block4)\n            tmp_block2 = self.shiftrows(tmp_block1)\n            tmp_block3 = self.mixcolumns(tmp_block2)\n            tmp_block4 = self.addroundkey(round_keys[i], tmp_block3)\n\n\n        # Final round\n        print(\"  Final round.\")\n        tmp_block1 = self.subbytes(tmp_block4)\n        tmp_block2 = self.shiftrows(tmp_block1)\n        tmp_block3 = self.addroundkey(round_keys[num_rounds], tmp_block2)\n\n        return tmp_block3\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # inv_mixw()\n    #\n    # Perform inverse bit mixing of the given words.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def inv_mixw(self, w):\n        (b0, b1, b2, b3) = self.w2b(w)\n\n        mb0 = self.gm14(b0) ^ self.gm11(b1) ^ self.gm13(b2) ^ self.gm09(b3)\n        mb1 = self.gm09(b0) ^ self.gm14(b1) ^ self.gm11(b2) ^ self.gm13(b3)\n        mb2 = self.gm13(b0) ^ self.gm09(b1) ^ self.gm14(b2) ^ self.gm11(b3)\n        mb3 = self.gm11(b0) ^ self.gm13(b1) ^ self.gm09(b2) ^ self.gm14(b3)\n\n        return self.b2w(mb0, mb1, mb2, mb3)\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # inv_mixcolumns()\n    #\n    # AES Inverse MixColumns on the given block.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def inv_mixcolumns(self, block):\n        (c0, c1, c2, c3) = block\n\n        mc0 = self.inv_mixw(c0)\n        mc1 = self.inv_mixw(c1)\n        mc2 = self.inv_mixw(c2)\n        mc3 = self.inv_mixw(c3)\n\n        res_block = (mc0, mc1, mc2, mc3)\n\n        if (self.VERBOSE):\n            print(\"Inverse MixColumns block in and block out:\")\n            self.print_block(block)\n            self.print_block(res_block)\n            print(\"\")\n\n        return res_block\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # inv_shiftrows()\n    #\n    # AES inverse ShiftRows block operation.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def inv_shiftrows(self, block):\n        (w0, w1, w2, w3) = block\n\n        c0 = self.w2b(w0)\n        c1 = self.w2b(w1)\n        c2 = self.w2b(w2)\n        c3 = self.w2b(w3)\n\n        ws0 = self.b2w(c0[0], c3[1],  c2[2],  c1[3])\n        ws1 = self.b2w(c1[0], c0[1],  c3[2],  c2[3])\n        ws2 = self.b2w(c2[0], c1[1],  c0[2],  c3[3])\n        ws3 = self.b2w(c3[0], c2[1],  c1[2],  c0[3])\n\n        res_block = (ws0, ws1, ws2, ws3)\n\n        if (self.VERBOSE):\n            print(\"Inverse ShiftRows block in and block out:\")\n            self.print_block(block)\n            self.print_block(res_block)\n            print(\"\")\n\n        return res_block\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # inv_subbytes()\n    #\n    # AES inverse SubBytes operation on the given block.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def inv_subbytes(self, block):\n        (w0, w1, w2, w3) = block\n\n        res_block = (self.inv_substw(w0), self.inv_substw(w1),\n                     self.inv_substw(w2), self.inv_substw(w3))\n\n        if (self.VERBOSE):\n            print(\"Inverse SubBytes block in and block out:\")\n            self.print_block(block)\n            self.print_block(res_block)\n            print(\"\")\n\n        return res_block\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # aes_decipher()\n    #\n    # Perform AES decipher operation for the given block\n    # using the given key length.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def aes_decipher_block(self, key, block):\n        tmp_block = block[:]\n\n        # Get round keys based on the given key.\n        if len(key) == 4:\n            round_keys = self.key_gen128(key)\n            num_rounds = self.AES_128_ROUNDS\n        else:\n            round_keys = self.key_gen256(key)\n            num_rounds = self.AES_256_ROUNDS\n\n            # Initial round\n        print(\"  Initial, partial round.\")\n        tmp_block1 = self.addroundkey(round_keys[len(round_keys) - 1], tmp_block)\n        tmp_block2 = self.inv_shiftrows(tmp_block1)\n        tmp_block4 = self.inv_subbytes(tmp_block2)\n\n        # Main rounds\n        for i in range(1 , (num_rounds)):\n            print(\"\")\n            print(\"  Round %02d\" % i)\n            print(\"  ---------\")\n\n            tmp_block1 = self.addroundkey(round_keys[(len(round_keys) - i - 1)], tmp_block4)\n            tmp_block2 = self.inv_mixcolumns(tmp_block1)\n            tmp_block3 = self.inv_shiftrows(tmp_block2)\n            tmp_block4 = self.inv_subbytes(tmp_block3)\n\n        # Final round\n        print(\"  Final AddRoundKeys round.\")\n        res_block = self.addroundkey(round_keys[0], tmp_block4)\n\n        return res_block\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # test_mixcolumns()\n    #\n    # Test the mixcolumns and inverse mixcolumns operations using\n    # some simple test values.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def test_mixcolumns(self):\n        nist_aes128_key = (0x2b7e1516, 0x28aed2a6, 0xabf71588, 0x09cf4f3c)\n\n        print(\"Test of mixcolumns and inverse mixcolumns:\")\n        mixresult = self.mixcolumns(nist_aes128_key)\n        inv_mixresult = self.inv_mixcolumns(mixresult)\n\n        print(\"Test of mixw ochi inv_mixw:\")\n        testw = 0xdb135345\n        expw  = 0x8e4da1bc\n        mixresult = self.mixw(testw)\n        inv_mixresult = self.inv_mixw(mixresult)\n        print(\"Testword:   0x%08x\" % testw)\n        print(\"expexted:   0x%08x\" % expw)\n        print(\"mixword:    0x%08x\" % mixresult)\n        print(\"invmixword: 0x%08x\" % inv_mixresult)\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # single_aes_test()\n    #\n    # Perform a single AES operation and check result\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def single_aes_test(self, title, encdec, key, block, expected):\n        print(title)\n        self.print_key(key)\n        self.print_block(block)\n\n        if encdec == \"encipher\":\n            result = self.aes_encipher_block(key, block)\n        else:\n            result = self.aes_decipher_block(key, block)\n        return self.check_block(result, expected)\n\n\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # test_aes()\n    #\n    # Test the AES implementation with 128 and 256 bit keys.\n    #-------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def test_aes(self):\n        nist_aes128_key = (0x2b7e1516, 0xe5fbc119, 0x1a0a52ef)\n        nist_plaintext3 = (0xf69f2445, 0xdf4f9b17, 0xad2b417b, 0xe66c3710)\n\n        nist_exp128_0 = (0x3ad77bb4, 0x0d7a3660, 0xa89ecaf3, 0x2466ef97)\n        nist_exp128_1 =        nist_exp256_0 = (0xf3eed1bd, 0xb5d2a03c, 0x064b5a7e, 0x3db181f8)\n        nist_exp256_1 = (0x591ccb10, 0xd410ed26, 0xdc5ba74a, 0x31362870)\n        nist_exp256_2 = (0xb6ed21b9, 0x9ca6f4f9, 0xf153e7b1, 0xbeafed1d)\n        nist_exp256_3 = (0x23304b7a, 0x39f9f3ff, 0x067d8d8f, 0x9e24ecc7)\n\n        tc_errors = 0\n        tc        = 0\n\n        if (self.VERBOSE):\n            print(\" AES-256.\", \"encipher\",\n         nist_aes256_key, nist_plaintext0, nist_exp256_0)\n        tc += 1\n\n        tc_errors += self.single_aes_test(\"Test 1 for AES-256.\", \"encipher\",\n     print(\"\")\n        print(\"   AES Decipher tests\")\n        print(\"   ==================\")\n        tc_errors += self.single_aes_test(\"Test 0 for AES-128.\", \"decipher\",\n         nist_aes128_key, nist_exp128_0, nist_plaintext0)\n        tc += 1\n\n        tc_errors += self.single_aes_test(\"Test 1 for AES-128.\", \"decipher\",\n         nist_aes128_key, nist_exp128_1, //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Internal constant and parameter definitions.\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  parameter DEBUG     = 0;\n\n  parameter CLK_HALF_PERIOD = 1;\n  parameter CLK_PERIOD      = 2 * CLK_HALF_PERIOD;\n\n  // The DUT address map.\n  parameter ADDR_NAME0       = 8'h00;\n  parameter ADDR_NAME1       = 8'h01;\n  parameter ADDR_VERSION     = 8'h02;\n\n  parameter ADDR_CTRL        = 8'h08;\n  parameter CTRL_INIT_BIT    = 0;\n  parameter CTRL_NEXT_BIT    = 1;\n  parameter CTRL_ENCDEC_BIT  = 2;\n  parameter CTRL_KEYLEN_BIT  = 3;\n\n  parameter ADDR_STATUS      = 8'h09;\n  parameter STATUS_READY_BIT = 0;\n  parameter STATUS_VALID_BIT = 1;\n\n  parameter ADDR_CONFIG      = 8'h0a;\n\n  parameter ADDR_KEY0        = 8'h10;\n  parameter ADDR_KEY1        = 8'h11;\n  parameter ADDR_KEY2        = 8'h12;\n  parameter ADDR_KEY3        = 8'h13;\n  parameter ADDR_KEY4        = 8'h14;\n  parameter ADDR_BLOCK2      = 8'h22;\n  parameter ADDR_BLOCK3      = 8'h23;\n\n  parameter ADDR_RESULT0     = 8'h30;\n  parameter ADDR_RESULT1     = 8'h31;\n  parameter ADDR_RESULT2     = 8'h32;\n  parameter //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Register and Wire declarations.\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  reg [31 : 0]  cycle_ctr;\n  reg [31 : 0]  error_ctr;\n  reg [31 : 0]  tc_ctr;\n\n  reg [31 : 0]  read_data;\n  reg [127 : 0] result_data;\n\n  reg           tb_clk;\n  reg           tb_reset_n;\n  reg           tb_cs;\n  reg           tb_we;\n  reg [7  : 0]  tb_address;\n  reg [31 : 0]  tb_write_data;\n  wire [31 : 0] tb_read_data;\n\n\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Device Under Test.\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  aes dut(\n           .clk(tb_clk),\n           .reset_n(tb_reset_n),\n           .cs(tb_cs),\n           .we(tb_we),\n           .address(tb_address),\n           .write_data(tb_write_data),\n           .read_data(tb_read_data)\n          );\n\n\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // clk_gen\n  //\n  // Always running clock generator process.\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  always\n    begin : clk_gen\n      #CLK_HALF_PERIOD;\n      tb_clk = !tb_clk;\n    end // clk_gen\n\n\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // sys_monitor()\n  //\n  // An always running process that creates a cycle counter and\n  // conditionally displays information about the DUT.\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  always\n    begin : sys_monitor\n      cycle_ctr = cycle_ctr + 1;\n\n      #(CLK_PERIOD);\n\n      if (DEBUG)\n        begin\n          dump_dut_state();\n        end\n    end\n\n\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // dump_dut_state()\n  //\n  // Dump the state of the dump when needed.\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  task dump_dut_state;\n    begin\n      $display(\"cycle: 0x%016x\", cycle_ctr);\n      $display(\"State of DUT\");\n      $display(\"------------\");\n      $display(\"ctrl_reg:   init   = 0x%01x, next   = 0x%01x\", dut.init_reg, dut.next_reg);\n      $display(\"config_reg: encdec = 0x%01x, length = 0x%01x \", dut.encdec_reg, dut.keylen_reg);\n      $display(\"\");\n\n      $display(\"block: 0x%08x, 0x%08x, 0x%08x, 0x%08x\",\n               dut.block_reg[0], dut.block_reg[1], dut.block_reg[2], dut.block_reg[3]);\n      $display(\"\");\n\n    end\n  endtask // dump_dut_state\n\n\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // reset_dut()\n  //\n  // Toggle reset to put the DUT into a well known state.\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  task reset_dut;\n    begin\n      $display(\"*** Toggle reset.\");\n      tb_reset_n = 0;\n\n      #(2 * CLK_PERIOD);\n      tb_reset_n = 1;\n      $display(\"\");\n    end\n  endtask // reset_dut\n\n\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // display_test_results()\n  //\n  // Display the accumulated test results.\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  task display_test_results;\n    begin\n      if (error_ctr == 0)\n        begin\n          $display(\"*** All %02d test cases completed successfully\", tc_ctr);\n        end\n      else\n        begin\n          $display(\"*** %02d tests completed - %02d test cases did not complete successfully.\",\n                   tc_ctr, error_ctr);\n        end\n    end\n  endtask // display_test_results\n\n\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // init_sim()\n  //\n  // Initialize all counters and testbed functionality as well\n  // as setting the DUT inputs to defined values.\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  task init_sim;\n    begin\n      cycle_ctr     = 0;\n      error_ctr     = 0;\n      tc_ctr        = 0;\n\n      tb_clk        = 0;\n      tb_reset_n    = 1;\n\n      tb_cs         = 0;\n      tb_we         = 0;\n      tb_address    = 8'h0;\n      tb_write_data = 32'h0;\n    end\n  endtask // init_sim\n\n\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // write_word()\n  //\n  // Write the given word to the DUT using the DUT interface.\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  task write_word(input [11 : 0] address,\n                  input [31 : 0] word);\n    begin\n      if (DEBUG)\n        begin\n          $display(\"*** Writing 0x%08x to 0x%02x.\", word, address);\n          $display(\"\");\n        end\n\n      tb_address = address;\n      tb_write_data = word;\n      tb_cs = 1;\n      tb_we = 1;\n      #(2 * CLK_PERIOD);\n      tb_cs = 0;\n      tb_we = 0;\n    end\n  endtask // write_word\n\n\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // write_block()\n  //\n  // Write the given block to the dut.\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  task write_block(input [127 : 0] block);\n    begin\n      write_word(ADDR_BLOCK0, block[127  :  96]);\n      write_word(ADDR_BLOCK1, block[95   :  64]);\n      write_word(ADDR_BLOCK2, block[63   :  32]);\n      write_word(ADDR_BLOCK3, block[31   :   0]);\n    end\n  endtask // write_block\n\n\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // read_word()\n  //\n  // Read a data word from the given address in the DUT.\n  // the word read will be available in the global variable\n  // read_data.\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  task read_word(input [11 : 0]  address);\n    begin\n      tb_address = address;\n      tb_cs = 1;\n      tb_we = 0;\n      #(CLK_PERIOD);\n      read_data = tb_read_data;\n      tb_cs = 0;\n\n      if (DEBUG)\n        begin\n          $display(\"*** Reading 0x%08x from 0x%02x.\", read_data, address);\n          $display(\"\");\n        end\n    end\n  endtask // read_word\n\n\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // read_result()\n  //\n  // Read the result block in the dut.\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  task read_result;\n    begin\n      read_word(ADDR_RESULT0);\n      result_data[127 : 096] = read_data;\n      read_word(ADDR_RESULT1);\n      result_data[095 : 064] = read_data;\n      read_word(ADDR_RESULT2);\n      result_data[063 : 032] = read_data;\n      read_word(ADDR_RESULT3);\n      result_data[031 : 000] = read_data;\n    end\n  endtask // read_result\n\n\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // init_key()\n  //\n  // init the key in the dut by writing the given key and\n  // key length and then trigger init processing.\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  task init_key(input [255 : 0] key, input key_length);\n    begin\n      if (DEBUG)\n        begin\n          $display(\"key length: 0x%01x\", key_length);\n          $display(\"Initializing key expansion for key: 0x%016x\", key);\n        end\n\n      write_word(ADDR_KEY0, key[255  : 224]);\n      write_word(ADDR_KEY1, key[223  : 192]);\n      write_word(ADDR_KEY2, key[191  : 160]);\n      write_word(ADDR_KEY3, key[159  : 128]);\n      write_word(ADDR_KEY4, key[127  :  96]);\n      write_word(ADDR_KEY5, key[95   :  64]);\n      write_word(ADDR_KEY6, key[63   :  32]);\n      write_word(ADDR_KEY7, key[31   :   0]);\n\n      if (key_length)\n        begin\n          write_word(ADDR_CONFIG, 8'h02);\n        end\n      else\n        begin\n          write_word(ADDR_CONFIG, 8'h00);\n        end\n\n      write_word(ADDR_CTRL, 8'h01);\n\n      #(100 * CLK_PERIOD);\n    end\n  endtask // init_key\n\n\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // ecb_mode_single_block_test()\n  //\n  // Perform ECB mode encryption or decryption single block test.\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  task ecb_mode_single_block_test(input [7 : 0]   tc_number,\n                                  input           encdec,\n                                  input [255 : 0] key,\n                                  input           key_length,\n                                  input [127 : 0] block,\n                                  input [127 : 0] expected);\n    begin\n      $display(\"*** TC %0d ECB mode test started.\", tc_number);\n      tc_ctr = tc_ctr + 1;\n\n      init_key(key, key_length);\n      write_block(block);\n      dump_dut_state();\n\n      write_word(ADDR_CONFIG, (8'h00 + (key_length << 1)+ encdec));\n      write_word(ADDR_CTRL, 8'h02);\n\n      #(100 * CLK_PERIOD);\n\n      read_result();\n\n      if (result_data == expected)\n        begin\n          $display(\"*** TC %0d successful.\", tc_number);\n          $display(\"\");\n        end\n      else\n        begin\n          $display(\"*** ERROR: TC %0d NOT successful.\", tc_number);\n          $display(\"Expected: 0x%032x\", expected);\n          $display(\"Got:      0x%032x\", result_data);\n          $display(\"\");\n\n          error_ctr = error_ctr + 1;\n        end\n    end\n  endtask // ecb_mode_single_block_test\n\n\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // aes_test()\n  //\n  // Main test task will perform complete NIST test of AES.\n  // Test vectors copied from the follwing NIST documents.\n  //\n  // NIST SP 800-38A:\n  // http://csrc.nist.gov/publications/nistpubs/800-38a/sp800-38a.pdf\n  //\n  // NIST FIPS-197, Appendix C:\n  // https://nvlpubs.nist.gov/nistpubs/FIPS/NIST.FIPS.197.pdf\n  //----------------------------------------------------------------\n  task aes_test;\n    reg [255 : 0] nist_aes128_key1;\n    reg [255 : 0] nist_ecb_256_enc_expected1;\n    reg [127 : 0] nist_ecb_256_enc_expected2;\n    reg [127 : 0] nist_ecb_256_enc_expected3;\n    reg [127 : 0] nist_ecb_256_enc_expected4;\n\n    begin\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-6","id":"msg_01CnpTMMLF4YnyQieZ6aHeK9","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Now I have a thorough understanding of all the interfaces. Let me implement all 7 RTL files. I'll start from the bottom up.\n\nThe key observations from the testbenches:\n\n1. **`aes_sbox`**: Takes `sboxw[31:0]` and outputs `new_sboxw[31:0]`. Each byte gets substituted. Also has internal signals `tmp_new_sbox0/1/2/3`.\n\n2. **`aes_inv_sbox`**: Same interface as sbox but uses inverse S-box.\n\n3. **`aes_encipher_block`**: \n\n\nThis is the main control module with clock, reset, and control signals. It manages the encryption state through registers tracking the current round and word counter, maintains four block word registers that get updated each cycle, and instantiates the round logic submodule to perform the actual encryption transformations on the block data.\n\nThe decryption module mirrors this structure but uses inverse operations—it has its own control registers and state tracking, plus a temporary sbox output register to handle the decryption-specific transformations like inverse shift rows and inverse mix columns before the key addition step.\n\nNow I'm looking at the key memory module, which manages the round key generation and storage.
